## Surfaced in Story 11.2 (bmad-build-auto, 2026-10-17)

- **The cost of glass (design question (a)) is not measured: profile-mode frame times over a scrolling 10k graph with the six islands need 10.1's synthetic recording and 11.3's host.** Medium.
  - epics.md Story 11.2 Design status (a) and its witness "In profile mode, frame build and raster times over a scrolling 10k graph with six islands are recorded, not gated"; `sprint-status.yaml` lists `10-1-graph-viewport-2d-virtualized` and `11-3-floating-overlay-host` as backlog.
  - **Follow-up:** once 10.1 and 11.3 land, measure in profile mode with the three top-bar islands, the rail, the inspector and the minimap open, record the numbers in that story's notes; a missed frame budget goes to Ralph (solid while scrolling, or fewer blurred surfaces).
- **Fading a glass surface through an Opacity ancestor drops its backdrop blur for the whole fade, and 11.3's calm scrolling plus the panel, toast and minimap entrances fade glass.** Medium.
  - Engine reading, not run on a device: Impeller opens an offscreen subpass for an opacity layer that holds a backdrop filter (`engine/src/flutter/impeller/display_list/canvas.cc` `SaveLayer`, :1830-1912), and `FlipBackdrop` reads only that subpass's texture (`render_passes_.back()`), which is still empty; `ZsSheet` (Story 11.2) fades its scrim through the filter's alpha instead.
  - **Follow-up:** 11.3 (calm scrolling, panel-in), 10.16 and 10.23 fade glass the same way (alpha in the filter's colour matrix plus an opacity on the content inside the `BackdropFilter`), e.g. through an opacity input on `ZsGlass`; confirm the behaviour on a Mac first.
  - **2026-10-18, Story 11.3:** `ZsFade` is the one way to fade glass — each `ZsSurface` under it puts its box under an `Opacity` inside the filter, gives the filter the alpha a(1 − Lα)/(1 − a·Lα) and fades its drops by colour; calm scrolling, the panel's entrance and exit, the flyout, the minimap slot's motion and the stale notice use it; confirmed on a Mac in the running catalogue (through `ZsFade` the backdrop stays blurred, under an `Opacity` ancestor it turned sharp) (commit 7ca412b); left: 10.16 (minimap) and 10.23 (toast) fade their own glass through `ZsFade`.
- **The DS drop menu enters on its own keyframe (--dur-pop, scale .94), while the epics' design update lists it among the command menus (--dur-menu, scale .97); 11.2 follows the epics.** Low.
  - DS `components/menus/menus.css:40-42` (`zs-drop-in`) against epics.md Story 11.2 design update, item 3 Entrances ("context, project, drop and text menu … `--dur-menu` 110 … .97"); a decision recorded in the epic wins over the DS.
  - **Follow-up:** Ralph confirms at 10.10 which entrance the drop menu takes, or the DS drops its own keyframe.
- **The callout arrow exists only where the DS draws it (the left edge, 16 px from the top); a callout that 11.3 flips, or the commit and gap callouts of 10.6 and 10.19, may need another side.** Low.
  - DS `components/surfaces/surfaces.css:17-18` defines only the left arrow and `.no-arrow`; flipping and anchoring are 11.3's (epics.md Story 11.2 non-goals); `app/lib/src/shared/materials/zs_menu_material.dart` takes `arrow: bool`.
  - **Follow-up:** 11.3 adds the arrow side and offset together with the placement and flip.
  - **2026-10-18, Story 11.3:** rejected for 11.3's part (spec-11-3-floating-overlay-host, no code): its one callout, the rail preview, sits at a fixed place right of its button with the arrow tip on the button's centre and its height bounded by the window, so it never flips or shifts and needs no other side or offset; left: the commit and gap callouts of 10.6 and 10.19.
- ~~**Focus goes nowhere after a sheet when the node that had focus as it opened has unmounted meanwhile, for example a sheet opened from a context-menu row whose menu then finishes its exit.**~~ — RESOLVED 2026-10-18 (spec-11-11-zs-control-library-widgetbook, commit ff1ccc6): `ZsMenuAnchor` refocuses its opener before the command runs, so `ZsSheet` records the opener, not the leaving row; a test checks focus on the opener before the dialog and again after Cancel; `ZsSheet` unchanged. Medium (unverified).
  - `app/lib/src/shared/materials/zs_sheet.dart:116` records `FocusManager.instance.primaryFocus` when the sheet opens and returns focus only to that node if it is still mounted; if 11.11's Menu still holds focus in its row when the command opens a ConfirmDialog, the row unmounts after the menu's 120 ms exit (review pass 2026-10-17, edge-case hunter).
  - **Follow-up:** 11.11 checks that the Menu returns focus to its opener when it starts closing, before the command runs; if it cannot, `ZsSheet` falls back to a mounted ancestor or takes a return node from its caller.
- ~~**The glass nesting assertion walks the element tree, so glass mounted through an OverlayPortal under an island (a panel pinned from a rail button) trips it although nothing overlaps.**~~ — REJECTED 2026-10-18 (spec-11-3-floating-overlay-host): no glass of the overlay host mounts through an `OverlayPortal` inside an island (the preview is the menu material, the pinned panel and the flyout are siblings of the rail in the shell's stack), so the check stays on the element tree; `ZsGlass`'s doc makes it the rule, and a shell test with a pinned panel, the flyout, an open preview and glass in every slot stays quiet. Medium (unverified).
  - `app/lib/src/shared/materials/zs_glass.dart:79` uses `context.findAncestorWidgetOfExactType<ZsGlass>()`; an `OverlayPortal`'s overlay child is an element child of the portal, so the island that hosts the portal counts as an ancestor (review pass 2026-10-17, blind hunter).
  - **Follow-up:** 11.3 decides whether any glass mounts through an `OverlayPortal` inside an island; if so, the nesting check moves to the render tree.
- **An open sheet does not stop the window's other shortcuts and menu items, so a command could act on the project window behind an open ConfirmDialog or Push.** Medium.
  - `app/lib/src/shared/materials/zs_sheet.dart` binds only Esc inside its `FocusScope`; key events bubble from the focused node to the `Shortcuts` above the sheet, and `PlatformMenuBar` items act regardless of focus. No window shortcut or menu item exists before 11.13 (review pass 2026-10-17, blind hunter).
  - **Follow-up:** 11.13 disables or routes the window's shortcuts and menu items while a sheet shows (for example through a modal flag the sheet publishes).

## Surfaced 2026-10-17 while reviewing where 11.1 and 11.2 put their code (console session, Ralph)

_Ralph asked whether the design, theme and UI code that 11.1 and 11.2 put in `app/lib/src/shared/` should live in packages of their own. He decided yes: Story 11.14 moves the tokens, the materials and the DS icons into `packages/zanshin_ui` before 11.11, and 11.11 builds its controls there. It supersedes 11.1's design question (a) ("a package only once a second consumer outside the app appears"). Epics.md and sprint-status.yaml carry it; what follows is only what 11.14 leaves open._

- **`zanshin_ui` depends on `zanshin_settings` and `zanshin_shell`.** Low; not in 11.14's scope (its non-goal).
  - `zs_appearance.dart` reads `AccentSource` from the settings package and `ShellTheme` from the shell package, and `zs_root.dart` reads `ShellTheme`. So a change to either package reruns the UI package's gate steps, and the design layer knows the settings model.
  - **Follow-up:** if it ever hurts, the appearance scope takes plain values (an accent-source enum of its own, a theme snapshot), and the app maps the settings and the shell onto them.
- **Older paths in this file.** Info. The "Surfaced in Story 11.1" and "Surfaced in Story 11.2" sections cite `app/lib/src/shared/design/`, `materials/` and `widgets/`. After 11.14 those files are in `packages/zanshin_ui/lib/src/design/`, `materials/` and `icons/`; read the old paths that way. The sections stay as written.

- ~~**The gate-plan rules list each Dart package's dependents by hand, and nothing checks them against the pubspecs, so a new path dependency makes the gate under-plan without an error.**~~ — RESOLVED 2026-10-17 (spec-11-14-closing-gate-rules-and-font-licences, commit cc8f353): an inline test derives each workspace member's dependents from the pubspecs and checks every package rule against them; a changed member pubspec now plans every step. Medium.
  - `crates/xtask/src/tasks/gate_plan.rs` `RULES` hard-codes the dependents of `zanshin_settings`, the three shell packages and `zanshin_ui`; the inline test `every_package_zanshin_ui_depends_on_plans_zanshin_ui_and_the_app` hard-codes the same paths. Pre-existing since `d81ebd9`; 11.14 added one more package.
  - **Follow-up:** an inline test that reads the `path:` dependencies of `app/pubspec.yaml` and `packages/*/pubspec.yaml`, follows them transitively and asserts every package rule plans each dependent's group (the test module already reads `Makefile.toml` that way). Worth doing before 11.11 adds a dependency to `zanshin_ui`.

Medium (unverified).
  - The debug bundle's `NOTICES.Z` holds no OFL text; `packages/zanshin_ui/assets/fonts/*/OFL.txt` sit next to the fonts, but no `LICENSE` file or `LicenseRegistry` entry brings them in. Pre-existing before the move.
  - **Follow-up:** check whether the licence fields in the TTFs' name tables satisfy the OFL; if not, add a `LICENSE` to `packages/zanshin_ui` (flutter_tools collects package licences into the notices) or register the texts with `LicenseRegistry.addLicense`.

The OFL text now ships in the bundle's NOTICES, so the licence still travels with the app; OFL-FAQ 2.4/2.8 ask a modified font to keep its licence metadata.
  - **Follow-up:** re-run the subset with `--name-IDs='*'` (or `0-6,13,14`) from the full fonts in git history and note it in `SUBSET.txt`.
- **The fonts' OFL entries in the NOTICES belong to no package, so a licence page (`LicensePage`, `showAboutDialog`) would not list them.** Low.
  - The three `OFL.txt` files use CRLF line endings; Flutter's runtime parser (`services/binding.dart` `_parseLicenses`) splits a chunk's package names off at the first `\n\n`, finds none and files each OFL text under no package. The texts are in `NOTICES.Z` (the OFL's containment condition holds); the app has no licence page yet, and before the closing run the texts were not in the NOTICES at all.
  - **Follow-up:** with the story that adds a licence page or About box: register the texts under their family names (an LF copy headed by the name, or `LicenseRegistry.addLicense`).
- **The app bundles Yaru's Ubuntu and Ubuntu Mono fonts without the Ubuntu Font Licence in its NOTICES.** Medium.
  - The test bundle's `FontManifest.json` lists `packages/yaru/Ubuntu` and `packages/yaru/UbuntuMono`; the gunzipped `NOTICES.Z` holds no "UBUNTU FONT LICENCE". Ralph's fork (`../yaru.dart/assets/fonts/LICENCE.txt`) has the text but no `flutter: licenses:` entry. UFL 1.0 asks every copy to carry the notice and the licence, like OFL §2. Pre-existing.
  - **Follow-up:** Story 11.10 retires Yaru and its fonts; if Yaru stays past it, add `flutter: licenses: [assets/fonts/LICENCE.txt]` to the fork's pubspec.

## Surfaced in Story 11.11 (bmad-build-auto, 2026-10-17)

- **On a real Mac a busy control shows the arrow, not a busy cursor: the macOS embedder maps `SystemMouseCursors.progress` to the arrow.** Low.
  - `engine/src/flutter/shell/platform/darwin/macos/framework/Source/FlutterMouseCursorPlugin.mm:34-61` maps no `progress` or `wait`; they fall back to the arrow. The Zs controls set `progress` while busy (DS readme.md:106, :129); widget tests see it, the screen does not.
  - **Follow-up:** accept it, or draw a busy cursor natively (a custom `NSCursor` through the runner) if Ralph wants the DS's `progress` on screen.
- **VoiceOver on macOS may not read what the Zs controls put in semantics hints and tooltips (the disabled reason), nor a checkbox's mixed state or menu roles.** Medium (unverified).
  - Read from the engine, not run: `accessibility_bridge.cc:313-362` derives the AX role from flags only (`SemanticsRole` is never copied, :591-645), uses the hint only for the ignored-node check (:381-384); `ax_platform_node_mac.mm:585-596` (AXHelp) never reads the stored tooltip, and AXValue reports mixed as 0 (:617-623). 11.11 follows the DS and puts the disabled reason in the hint (DS components/base.css:38).
  - **Follow-up:** check a disabled-with-reason button, a mixed checkbox and a menu with Accessibility Inspector; if confirmed, carry the reason in the label or value, or fix it upstream.
- **The catalogue has no Graph folder until 10.1 adds it: 10.1's 2026-10-16 note covers only the case that 10.1 lands before 11.11.** Low.
  - epics.md:8151-8152; 10.1 is backlog and `GraphViewOptions` does not exist yet; the folder's only content is 10.1's demo host (decision 16.30).
  - **Follow-up:** 10.1 adds the folder "Graph" to `app/widgetbook/catalogue.dart`, its use cases join the smoke test.
- **material_ui's `TextField` paints no selection while unfocused, so a Zs text field never shows the unemphasized selection of an unfocused field.** Low.
  - `material_ui-1.5.0/lib/src/text_field.dart:1740` passes the selection colour only while focused; DS readme.md:177 asks for the unemphasized grey in an unfocused field. Inside an inactive window the focused field does show it.
  - **Follow-up:** none until a screen needs a kept selection in an unfocused field; then paint it in the well (`getBoxesForSelection`) or wrap `EditableText`.
- **The optional parity test "every component in DS `_ds_manifest.json` has a use case" is not built.** Low.
  - epics.md:12890 makes it optional; the manifest's 77 entries include the graph, search, push, settings and window components of later stories, so it would fail until they land.
  - **Follow-up:** add it with the last story that builds a manifest component (11.10 at the latest), or with a list of the entries still owned by open stories.
- **The rule "`marionette_mcp` must match `marionette_flutter` exactly" cannot be kept as written: the app allows `^0.7.0` and the documented install command takes the newest `marionette_mcp`.** Low. (Surfaced in the review.)
  - `marionette_mcp-0.7.0/lib/src/vm_service/vm_service_context.dart:110-125` refuses any other binding version; `app/pubspec.yaml:22` allows `^0.7.0` (the lockfile holds 0.7.0 until a `pub upgrade`), and `project-context.md:557-558` installs with a bare `dart pub global activate marionette_mcp`, so either side can move to 0.7.x alone and agents can no longer connect.
  - **Follow-up:** Ralph's call: pin `marionette_flutter` exactly, or document `dart pub global activate marionette_mcp <the lockfile's version>` and bump both together.

## Surfaced 2026-10-18 while comparing the Widgetbook catalogue with the DS cards (console session, Ralph)

_Ralph found four defects in the catalogue (Select chevron, a second scroll bar, the Skeleton loop, unlabelled Appearance fields; fixed in `088c577`). Claude then compared 17 of the 25 control families with the DS cards, rendered full size with headless Chrome, through Marionette (night, multicolor); the two control deviations and the catalogue gaps it found are fixed in `9c033ef` and `407df81`. Since then Claude checks every UI change that way (house rule "Tests", amendment 2026-10-18, `8b6b68d`). What follows is what the session left open._

- **Not yet compared with the DS: Link, Badge on its own, Kbd, Spinner, ProgressBar, Presence, and every family in paper, in an inactive window and under increased contrast.** Low.
  - The 2026-10-18 comparison covered the other 17 families in night with the multicolor accent only.
  - **Follow-up:** compare them the same way (Marionette `zanshinCatalogue.open` with `theme` / `inactive` / `contrast`, against the DS cards).
- **The field list's danger row is highlighted in the blue-grey `--fl-on`, while the DS menu's danger row highlights in red (`dangerHover`).** Low (unverified).
  - Seen in the catalogue's "Field list" state matrix; whether the field-attached list follows the menu's danger rule was not checked against the DS.
  - **Follow-up:** check the DS rule for a field-attached list's danger row; align `ZsFieldList` if it follows the menu.
- **The DS Skeleton sweep jumps at every loop, and Zanshin deliberately deviates.** None; a record.
  - DS components/controls/controls.css:62, :67 moves the band only from `background-position:150%` to `-50%`, so half of it stays on the bar at both ends; `zsSkeletonBand` (`packages/zanshin_ui/lib/src/controls/zs_skeleton.dart`) lets it enter and leave whole (`088c577`).
  - **Follow-up:** mention it with the next Claude Design round.
- **Golden tests: Ralph asked whether goldens could compare against the DS and serve as extra tests.** Open; no decision.
  - Claude's proposal: goldens of the catalogue's state matrices (night and paper, real fonts loaded) as regression tests, plus a generated side-by-side sheet (DS card through headless Chrome next to the golden) for review, not as a gate; a pixel-exact comparison with the browser cannot work. It needs a story, because the house rule "Tests" ("no golden tests") and 11.11's non-goal say otherwise.
  - **Follow-up:** Ralph decides whether to write the story (or a spike with one component first).

## Surfaced in Story 11.3 (bmad-build-auto, 2026-10-18)

- **The calm state machine and the graph insets reach Epic 10 in a shape 10.5's text does not yet describe: the host counts wheel and trackpad scrolling over the graph layer itself, takes pans, flings and minimap scrolling through a report call, and hands the insets down through an inherited scope.** Low.
  - epics.md Story 10.5 items 14-15 (:8907-8920, :8943-8949) say the view model publishes `calm` and takes `GraphInsets` "as a listenable"; 11.3's witnesses (epics.md:12076-12080, :12172-12176) need the 30 px / 650 ms machine before 10.5 exists, so it lives in `CalmController` (`app/lib/src/features/shell/calm_controller.dart`). A fling's ballistic glide after the fingers lift sends no pointer events, so calm can end while the graph still glides.
  - **Follow-up:** 10.5 reads the insets with `ShellScope.insetsOf(context)` and reports pans, flings and minimap scrolling with `ShellScope.of(context).reportUserScroll`; its text is reconciled to this shape when it is specced.
- ~~**The project window keeps two stand-ins for Story 11.4 until it lands: a WindowDragArea over the bar row and a canvasBase fill under the graph.**~~ — RESOLVED 2026-10-18 (spec-11-4-window-chrome-floating-layout, commit fdd9817): `HomePage` passes `ZsCanvas` as the canvas, and its `WindowDragArea` bar row is the window's drag region of design question (a) (decision (g)), no longer a stand-in. Low.
  - epics.md Story 11.3 item 1 and Story 11.4 own the drag region and the canvas; without the stand-ins the new home could not be moved (the Yaru page's `WindowDragArea` AppBar went with it) and the vibrancy view would show through (`MainWindowChrome.swift:90-110`).
  - **Follow-up:** 11.4 replaces `HomePage`'s `barRow` and passes the canvas slot (`app/lib/src/features/home/home_page.dart`).
- **The in-app theme switch disappears with the Yaru test page until Story 11.7 adds Theme to Settings; the app then follows the stored mode, System by default.** Low.
  - The Yaru page's `SegmentedButton` was the only UI caller of `ZanshinAppViewModel.setThemeMode` (`app/lib/src/features/app/zanshin_app.dart`); epics.md Story 11.3 item 1 replaces the page; 11.7 owns General › Theme.
  - **Follow-up:** 11.7 builds the Theme row on `setThemeMode`.
- **The pinned panel's title takes the token `--type-panel-title` (15/600) while the desktop kit draws 13/600; a design question for the next DS round.** Low.
  - DS tokens/typography.css:29 against DS ui_kits/desktop/kit.css:84-86 and the export's 13 px h3 (app.css:1174-1179); both are DS tokens or CSS, the same tier of precedence. `app/lib/src/features/shell/pinned_panel.dart` uses the token.
  - **Follow-up:** ask in the next Claude Design round which size the panel header takes.
- **A rail button loses its hover fill while the pointer is inside its preview, where the kit keeps it (`.is-hov`).** Low.
  - DS ui_kits/desktop/kit.css:66; `ZsControlCore._syncHover` (`packages/zanshin_ui/lib/src/controls/zs_control_core.dart:252`) rewrites `WidgetState.hovered` from the pointer, so no external hovered state survives; the epics list only `--fl-hover` and `--fl-on` (epics.md:12107).
  - **Follow-up:** if Ralph wants the kit's look, the core gains a way to hold a hover the pointer did not make (or the rail paints the fill itself).
- **No Zs menu, popover, select or sheet takes the shell's calm hold by itself, and `ZsSelect` and `ZsPopover` expose no open callback, so a slot whose story forgets `ShellScope.of(context).openSurface()` lets calm scrolling fade an open menu.** Medium. (Surfaced in the review.)
  - The hold is an explicit call (`ShellController.openSurface`; `app/lib/src/features/shell/shell_scope.dart`, `FloatingShell`'s "What a slot must bring"); `ZsMenuAnchor` has onOpen/onClose (`packages/zanshin_ui/lib/src/controls/zs_menu.dart:186-190`), `ZsPopover` only onClosed, `ZsSelect` none; an overlay child inherits its island's `ZsFade`, so an unheld menu fades with the island while it stays interactive.
  - **Follow-up:** 10.7, 10.21, 11.5 and 11.8 hold calm for each surface their slots open; `ZsSelect` gains open/close callbacks when a slot needs one.
- **A resize edge removed during its own drag never reports the drag's end, so the shell would keep its calm hold and its edge-drag flag until the window closes.** Medium (unverified). (Surfaced in the review.)
  - `ZsResizeEdge` (`packages/zanshin_ui/lib/src/controls/zs_resize_edge.dart`) calls onDragEnd only from the recognizer's end and cancel, and a recognizer disposed with its widget fires neither; in 11.3 nothing removes the panel edge during a mouse drag (a window resize, the inspector opening and an unpin each need a second input).
  - **Follow-up:** 10.8 checks whether its inspector can close while its edge is dragged (a deselect from elsewhere); if any path removes an edge mid-drag, `ZsResizeEdge` ends the drag in `dispose`.
- ~~**`HomePage` injects `ZanshinFfiVersion` and never reads it.**~~ — RESOLVED 2026-10-18 (spec-11-3-closing-home-page-unused-ffi-version, commit d96bb16): `HomePage` drops the parameter and `MainComponent` drops `ZanshinFfiModule`, whose only consumer it was. Low. (Surfaced in the review; pre-existing.)
  - `app/lib/src/features/home/home_page.dart:22-27`; before this story `HomePage` returned `const AppShell()` and did not read it either.
  - **Follow-up:** drop it with the next change to the home (11.10).

## Surfaced in the closing run of Story 11.3 (bmad-build-auto, 2026-10-18)

- **`ZanshinFfiModule`'s version wrapper never frees the Rust-allocated C string it reads, against `zanshin_ffi_version`'s caller-frees contract.** Low.
  - `packages/zanshin_core/lib/src/ffi/zanshin_version.dart:15` returns `bindings.zanshin_ffi_version().cast<Utf8>().toDartString`, a tear-off over a pointer never passed to `zanshin_string_free` (contract: `bindings.dart:792`, `crates/zanshin-ffi/src/lib.rs:65-72`, `docs/ffi-abi.md:28`). Pre-existing; since the closing run no production code calls it, only `app/test/native_asset_smoke_test.dart:12` and `packages/zanshin_core/test/ffi/zanshin_version_test.dart:26`.
  - **Follow-up:** copy the string and free the pointer in the wrapper, or retire `ZanshinFfiModule` in favour of `GetDiagnostics` (`diagnostics.dart:15-29` already carries `appVersion` and `gitSha` and frees its buffer); the raw-path test in `zanshin_version_test.dart:36-47` stays the extern fn's coverage witness either way.
- **Two DI bindings in `MainComponent`, `GetDiagnostics` and `IconRegistry`, are registered but consumed by nothing, so every build_runner run in `app/` prints two unused-binding warnings.** Low.
  - The regenerated `app/lib/main.inject.dart` builds `getDiagnostics$Provider` and `iconRegistry$Provider` and hands them to nothing (modules at `app/lib/main.dart:118-124`); the warnings were also in Story 11.3's build_runner output. Pre-existing.
  - **Follow-up:** the first story that consumes diagnostics or the registry through DI wires it; otherwise drop the unconsumed modules from the component, so a new unused-binding warning stands out.

## Surfaced in Story 11.4 (bmad-build-auto, 2026-10-18)

- **AppKit posts no notification when entering full screen fails, so the window chrome watch takes a full-screen entry that has not arrived after 2 s as failed.** Low.
  - The spec's trigger list (spec-11-4-window-chrome-floating-layout, Design Notes "Measurement") names `didFailToEnterFullScreen`; `NSWindow` has no such notification (only the delegate's `windowDidFailToEnterFullScreen(_:)`, and the engine owns the delegate), and `flutter build macos` failed on it. `WindowChromeHost.swift` clears its entering flag on `didEnterFullScreen` / `didExitFullScreen` and, failing both, after `enterFullScreenTimeout` (2 s, Claude's proposal).
  - **Follow-up:** Ralph's full-screen check (⌃⌘F in and out) covers the normal path; if a failed entry ever shows the bar at 12 for those 2 s, or the engine starts forwarding window-delegate calls, switch to the delegate.
- **The native half of Story 11.4 is verified only by `flutter build macos --debug`, one live block report and the asset test: the rings' colour and theme switch, full screen, the opaque rounded window, the 880 × 560 drag stop and Multicolor's native tint were not seen from this seat.** Medium.
  - No Swift test target exists (see the Story 11.1 entry above on `Package.swift`), and this seat has neither screen recording nor accessibility; the running app's log reported the block x 19–79, centre y 26, for view 2, and Marionette read the bar at 91 / 4–48 and the drag row at 0–60. This Mac runs the Orange accent, so the AccentColor exact-match item above stays unverified.
  - **Follow-up:** Ralph's manual checks as listed in spec-11-4-window-chrome-floating-layout § Verification; later a Swift test target or a runtime smoke check.
- ~~**The catalogue's windows wear the app's chrome but their roots have no `WindowChromeBuilder`, so the real Windows › Floating shell window lays the bar out from the reference geometry and ignores full screen.**~~ — RESOLVED 2026-10-18 (spec-11-4-closing-catalogue-window-chrome, commit 84a47af): `CatalogueWindowRoot` wraps the catalogue windows' root in `WindowChromeBuilder`, `catalogueAppBuilder` copies full screen and the block into its `ZsRoot`, and `FloatingShellDemo` no longer forces `fullScreen: false`. Low. (Surfaced in the review.)
  - `app/widgetbook/folders/windows.dart` opens it through `AppWindows.create` (`chrome: true`, `app/lib/src/features/window/app_windows.dart`); its root is `catalogueAppBuilder`, a `ZsRoot` without the builder (`app/widgetbook/catalogue.dart`), so in full screen the first island stays at 91 / 4 and the drag row at 60.
  - **Follow-up:** 11.12's per-window roots wrap every window's `ZsRoot` in `WindowChromeBuilder`, the catalogue's included.
